ceṉṉai-1 


patippurai 


paḻan tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip potuvāka, “pattup pāṭṭu”, 
“eṭṭut kokai, “patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku” eṉṟu pākupaṭutti 
uraippatu uraiyāciriyarkaḷ kālanṭutāṭṭup 
oru marapākum. “tamiḻ viṭu tūtiṉ' āciriyar, 
_— mūttōrkaḷ 
pāṭiyaruḷ pattup pāṭṭum, eṭṭut kokaiyum, 
kēṭu il patiṉeṭṭuk kīḻkkaṇakkum 


eṉṟu kuṟippiṭutalum im marapaip pulappaṭuttum. im muṟai 
vaippil “pattup pāṭṭu” caṅka kālat kokai nūlkaḷuḷ mutaṉ 
maiyāka vaittu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu pattup periya 81 
vaṟ pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭa kokuti. nīṇṭa akavaṟ pāṭalukku 
utāraṇamāka iḷampūraṇar pattup pāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷaiyum 
kuṟittuḷḷār. “periya pāṭṭu pattup pāṭṭiṉuḷḷum, cilappati 
kārattuḷḷum, maṇimēkalaiyuḷḷum kaṇṭu koḷka”? (kol. 
ceyyu. 150) eṉpatu avaratu vākku. 


pattup pāṭṭiṉ ēṭṭup piratikaḷil kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷa 
pāṭal oṉṟu it tokutiyil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷai muṟaippaṭut 
tik kūṟukiṉṟatu. appāṭṭu varumāṟu: 


muruku, porunāṟu, pāṇ iraṇṭu, mullai, 
paruku vaḷa maturaik kāñci, maruviṉiya 

kōla neṭunalvāṭai, kōl kuṟiñci, paṭṭiṉap 
pālai, kaṭātkoṭum, pattu. 


ip pāṭalil kuṟitta varicaiyilēyē pala piratikaḷilum pāṭal 


kaḷ amaintiruntaṉa. aṉṟiyum, mutalāvatu, iraṇṭāvatu, 
eṉa eṇṇup peyarālum ivai kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 


eṉiṉum, “pattup pāṭṭu eṉak kuṟikkum vaḻakkam mika 
muṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu koḷḷuvataṟku illai. ip peyarai iṉam 
pūraṇarum, mayilainātarumē (naṉ. 387), tattam uraikaḷil kuṟit 
tuḷḷaṉar. pērāciriyarō tamatu tolkāppiya uraiyil 
“pāṭṭu eṉṟē (ceyyu. 50, 80 urai) vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. 
iḷampūraṇajanam piṟa uraiyāciriyarkaḷum pattup pāṭṭup 
pāṭalkaḷai mēṟkōḷkaḷāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭumpoḻutu, av 


4 


tokai nūṟkaḷiṟpōla it tokutiyait tokuttār, 
tokuppittār paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ etuvum kiṭaikkap 
peṟavillai. kaṭaic caṅka varalāṟu kūṟumiṭattu, eṭṭut tatokai 
nūlkaḷai muṟaiyāka eṭuttu viḷakkum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ 


nūṟu aṭic ciṟumai, nūṟṟup pattu aḷavē. 

ēṟiya aṭiyiṉ īr-ōm pāṭṭuk 

mitākuppatu pattup pāṭṭu eṉappaṭumama. (384) 
atuvē, akavaliṉ varum eṉa aṟaikuvar pulavar. (385) 


intac cūttiraṅkaḷ “pattup pāṭṭu” eṉṉum vaḻakkup perukiya 
kālattil iyaṟṟap peṟṟavai. “pattup pāṭṭu ilakkiyattai 
nōkki vakukkappeṟṟakē inta ilakkaṇam. veṟu vakaiyāṉa 
pattup pāṭṭukkaḷ tamiḻulakil illai. 


pattup pāṭṭu nūl pattup pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭa oru 
kōvaiyāyiṉum, “kuṟuntokai', “puṟa nāṉūṟu” pōṉṟa nūṟ 


kōvai yaṉṟu. ivai oṉṟaṟku oṉṟu toṭarpu iṉṟi, vev 
vēṟu cūḻnilaiyil, vevvēṟu kālattil, eḻunta pāṭṭukkaḷ. 


ivaṟṟuḷ cempāti āṟṟuppaṭaikaḷ. avaiyāvaṉa: tiru 
muruku āṟṟuppaṭai, porunar āṟṟuppaṭai, ciṟupāṇ āṟ 
ṟuppaṭai, perumpāṇ āṟṟuppaṭai, malaipaṭukaṭām. tiru 
muruku āṟṟuppaṭaikkup “pulavar āṟṟuppaṭai” eṉṟa 
oru peyarum uṇṭu. malaipaṭukaṭām “kūttar āṟṟuppaṭai 
eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. “mullaip pāṭṭu”, “kuṟiñcippāṭṭu”, “paṭṭi 
ṉap pālai”, eṉṉum mūṉṟum akattiṇai yoḻukkam paṟṟiyaṉa. 


2 


pattup pāṭṭuḷ mikac ciṟiyatu mullaippāṭṭu; itil 103 
aṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mikap periya pāṭal maturaik kāñcu; itil 
uḷḷa aṭikaḷ 782. 


ovvoru pāṭalaiyum iyaṟṟiya pulavar peyarum, avarāl 
pāṭap peṟṟa pāṭṭuṭait talaivar peyarum, avvap pāṭal 
kaḷiṉ iṟutiyil koṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷa pamaiya vākkiyaṅkaḷāl 
pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭiṉap pālai, malaipaṭukaṭām nūlkaḷaip pāṭi 
ṉōr, avaṟṟiṉ pāṭṭuṭait kalaivārkaḷ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ 
piṟkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntum cilācāsaṉaṅkaḷiliruntum 
mitariyavarukiṉṟaṉa. 


pattup pāṭṭu muḻumaikkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟanta muṟai 
yil urai vakuttuḷḷār. 

cāṉṟōr uraitta taṇ tamiḻt teriyal 

orupatu pāṭṭum uṇāvārākku ellām 

uraiyaṟa muḻutum puraipaṭa uraittum 
eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraiyaip paṟṟiya ciṟappup pāyirap 
pakuti. 

pattup pāṭṭup pāṭaṟ piratikaḷiṉ īṟṟil cila kaṉi veṇ 
pākkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷ “taṉip pāṭalkaḷ” eṉṉum kalaip 
puṭaṉ antantap pāṭalkaḷiṉ īṟṟil amaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 
ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ poruṭpōkkai viḷakkum vakaiyil pāṭal 
kaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kulaippukkaḷ koṭuttu amaikkap 
peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avvap pāṭalkaḷil varum ciṟappup peyar muta 
liyavai kokukkappeṟṟu, akarāti varicaiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
iṅṅaṉamē, “naṟṟiṇai, “kuṟuntokai”, mutaliya tokai 
nūlkaḷilum “ciṟappup peyar mutaliyavai” iṭam 
peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. taviravum, tokai nūlkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum 
pāṭiṉēr pāṭappaṭṭōrkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ pāṭal eṇkaḷuṭaṉ 
akarāti varicaiyil amaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. “pattup pāṭṭu”, 
“eṭṭut tokaikaḷil” varum aruñ coṟkaḷukku uriya urai 
viḷakkamum, pulavar akarātiyum, ivaṟṟil payiṉṟu vantuḷḷa 
“pamaica kakaikaḷ”, piṟa varalāṟukaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ, aṟavuraikaḷ 
mutaliya kuṟippukkaḷum “pāṭṭum macakokaiyum' eṉṉum 
taṉit kokutiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. it kokuti caṅka 
nūṟkaḷ aṉaittiṟkum oru vaḻikāṭṭiyāy amaiyum. 


pāṭṭu. tokaikaḷiṉ patippup peru muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭu 
uḻaitta tamiḻp periyārkaḷum aṉparkaḷum palarāvar. avar 


kaḷuḷ' mutaṉmaiyāka naṉṟiyōṭu kuṟippiṭattakkavar pattup 


6 


pāṭṭaiyum, eṭṭut tokai nūlkaḷil aintaiyum cemmai 
yuṟa naṉku paricōtittu, tumimulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiya makāmakōpāt 


maṇiyam avarkaḷukku ematu naṉṟi uriyatākum. maṟṟum, aka 
nāṉūṟu, kalittokai, mutaliya ēṉaiya nūlkaḷaip paricōtittup 
patippitta sētu samskāṉa makāvitvāṉ pāṣā kavicēkarar 
rā. rākavaiyaṅkār, srī vatsa cakkaravartti rājakōpālaiyaṅkār 
tiru. ci. vai. tāmōtaram piḷḷa, tiru. i. vai. aṉantu 
rāmaiyar mutaliyavarkaḷum naṉṟiyuṭaṉ pārāṭṭat takkavarkaḷ. 
muṟpatippukkaḷ aṉaitkaiyum paṟpala ēṭṭup piratikaḷaiyum oppu 
nōkki, ettaṉaiyō tiruttaṅkaḷ kaṇṭu, pulavar varicaiyil 
caṅkap pāṭalkaḷai acciṭṭu, “caṅka ilakkiyam” eṉṟa peyaril 
ulavac ceyka pērāciriyar es. vaiyāpurip piḷḷai avarkaḷu 


tokaiḷil kaṇṭu makiḻvōmāka! paḻan tamiḻk karuvūlaṅkaḷā 
kiya ivaṟṟait tamiḻulakam viruppuṭaṉ ēṟṟup pōṟṟuvītāka!

murukāṟu, porunāṟu, ciṟupāṇāṟu, 
mullai, perumpāṇāṟu, maturaik kāñci, 
paritu āya poruḷ taḻuvā neṭunalvāṭai,

ōṅku peruñ celvam, icai, uṟṟu vāḻi. 


tiripu canti paṟṟiya oru kuṟippu 


tiripu cantiyil ōr eḻuttu piṟitu oṉṟāka māṟum. varumoḻiyil valliṉa 
mutalmoḻi varumpoḻutu, nilaimoḻi īṟṟil “ṉ”, “l”, iraṇṭum variṉ, “ṟ” āka 
vum, “ṇ”, “ḷ”, iraṇṭum variṉ, “ṭa” ākavum, vēṟupaṭum. maṟṟum “l”, “ḷ', iv 
iraṇṭiṉ muṉ melliṉa mutal moḻi vantāl, l “ṉ” ākavum, ḷ “ṇ” ākavum, 
māṟuvatu uṇṭu. eṉavē, nilmoḻic collaiṉ, l, ṇ,ḷ, ṉ ṇ oṉṟaik 
koṇṭa collāka iṭattiṟku ēṟṟapaṭi koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. urupukaḷ 
peyaruṭaṉ cērntu varum iṭaṅkaḷilum iv vakait tiripu cantikaḷil cala varum. 


mēlum, “m” īṟṟuca coṟkaḷ piṉvarum moḻikaḷukkut takkapaṭi, “ ka” mutal 
moḻi variṉ “ṅ” ākavum, “ca: mutal moḻi variṉ “ñ” ākavum, “ka” mutal moḻi 
variṉ “n” ākavum, iṉam otta melliṉamākat tirintu varum. (

cūraramakaḷiriṉ iyalpu

āṟṟuppaṭuttal 
cēvaṭi paṭarum cemmal uḷḷamoṭu, 
nalam puri koḷkaip pulam pirintu uṟaiyum, 
celavu nī nayantaṉaiāyiṉ, pala uṭaṉ, 
naṉṉar neñcattu iṉ nacai vāyppa, 65 
iṉṉē pepaṟuti, nī muṉṉiya viṉaiyē: 


tirupparaṅkuṉṟil murukaṉ vīṟṟiruttal 


cerup pukaṉṟu eṭutta cēṇ uyar neṭuṅ koṭi 

varip puṉai pankoṭu pāvai tūṅka, 

yorunart tēytta pōr aru vāyil, 

tiru vīṟṟirunta tatu tar niyamattu, 70 
māṭam mali maṟukiṉ kūṭaṟ kuṭavayiṉ— 

iruñ cēṟṟu akal vayal virintu vāy aviḻnta 

muḷ tāṭa tāmarait tuñci, vaikaṟaik 


vainnuti poruta vaṭu āḻ vari nutal 


vāṭā mālai ōṭaiyayōṭu tuyalvara, 

paṭu maṇi iraṭṭum maruṅkiṉ, kaṭu naṭai, 80 
kūṟṟattaṉṉa māṟṟu aru moympiṉ, 

kāl ḷerntaṉṉa vēḻam mēlkoṇṭu-— 


āṟu mukaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷ 


aivēṟu uruviṉ ceyviṉai muṟṟiya 
muṭiyoṭu viḷaṅkiya muraṇ miku tiru maṇi 
miṉ uṟaḻ imaippiṉ ceṉṉip poṟpa, uta 
nakai tāḻpu tuyalvarūum vakai amai polaṅ kuḻai 

cēṇ viḷaṅku iyaṟkai vāḷ mati kavaii

eruvai meṉ kōl koṇṭaṉir kaḻimiṉ.

nirai celal mel aṭi neṟi māṟu paṭukuvir.
